# Execution index

## Completion policy, November 17, 2026

Implementation-plan completion now means working local end-to-end behavior under [Section 2](mixer-product-plan.md#2-completion-definition-and-scope) and L01–L08 in [the local checklist](gates/local.json). The eight checks cover setup/import/export, shared app/UI/capabilities, compiler/VM, signed OTA/recovery, the real local console/API/database/worker/build workflow, free administration, security/local reliability, and FieldLog/documentation.

Testing and early launch are free. Payment and purchase implementation, Stripe integration, paid entitlements, invoices, and financial activation are deferred. Verify the workflow without payment credentials or a billing service. This plan edit does not itself implement or verify that behavior. Keep existing billing code and evidence.

[Section 29](mixer-product-plan.md#29-completion-gates-and-later-qualification) maps every G01–G38 gate to local evidence and later qualification. Live deployment, provider operations, physical phones, registry publication, store approval, external apps/customers, paid pilots, and commercial metrics do not block plan completion. Historical GA counts below describe the original launch checklist, not local completion. All eight local records start planned; no application test ran as part of this policy change and no earlier failure or hold is released.

Use Sol 6.2 High for all new subagents, including blocker experiments and independent reviews. Select `gpt-6.2-sol` with `reasoningEffort: "high"` and `serviceTier: "default"`. See [the model settings in Section 25](mixer-product-plan.md#controlling-model-and-subagent-configuration).

When a child stops before producing a result, follow the [delegated task recovery procedure](mixer-product-plan.md#when-a-delegated-task-stops).

The following checkpoints retain their historical sources and scope. Local completion is still pending; evaluate existing slice receipts against L01–L08 before claiming a pass.

## November 18 local verification

The full iOS calculation run stopped at installation with CoreSimulator Mach error -308. Root removed the disposable device after confirming Shutdown. The external comparison found one unrelated Minds Simulator changed from Booted to Shutdown. Mixer iOS runs are paused for recovery coordination. [Install and cleanup receipt](verification/receipts/fieldlog-ios-full-calculation-install-recovery-41a05264.json)

In the rebuilt shared-origin replay, both Android APKs installed and matched their pins. Activity Manager reported a launch timeout before the first FieldLog screen. The crash buffer contained repeated Android Bluetooth service failures. iOS ended its rendezvous wait, and the owned emulator, Simulator, private ADB and origin closed. Neither app reached a signed release phase. [Joint native attempt](verification/receipts/fieldlog-rebuilt-shared-native-launch-41a05264.json)

An owned API 36 emulator completed the ordinary FieldLog task conflict. After a 409 response, the user kept the local title **Inspect valve today** and the peer assignee **Morgan**. Both replicas reached version 3, each kept four prior outbox rows, and the final offline cold reopen preserved the Android workspace byte for byte. The earlier iOS replay reached the same task outcome with a separate workspace. [Android conflict receipt](verification/receipts/fieldlog-android-ordinary-task-conflict-41a05264.json)

The F05 design keeps the existing signed recovery authority and selects one closed V3 runtime owner. The exact guest exceeds the 4 MiB installed ceiling. Work proceeds on a compact signed-host control and a separate guest-size measurement. [Design decision](verification/receipts/fieldlog-f05-signed-design-decision-41a05264.json)

The F05 host probe compiled the historical FieldLog reducer and presentation to Swift/WASI and ran them on the owned development256 VM. Current edit, stale-action rejection and cancel checks passed. Retirement reported zero current usage in all fourteen ledger counters and zero live tickets or cleanup invariant failures. The probe used a candidate WASI build fix. Installed signed selection and device runs remain open. [F05 host receipt](verification/receipts/fieldlog-f05-public-swift-5961ea0c.json)

The L07 terminal restart attempt used a CLI that predates the current closure acknowledgement. The worker declined shutdown, leaving compiler custody unknown. Three separate no-compiler controls passed. [Worker custody receipt](verification/receipts/l07-worker-terminal-restart-hold-41a05264.json)

A restricted Oban build reached one durable worker start. The test stopped its owned worker before compiler spawn. Restart on the same journal observed uncertainty with no second start. The owner replayed the original build, tenant B received 404, and the coordinator retained capacity after lease expiry. Post-spawn closure and artifact readback remain open. [Worker recovery receipt](verification/receipts/l07-worker-recovery-07a536f3.json)

The starter follow-up makes a verified nonzero CLI exit before Xcode a terminal failed build. Both installed-worker test modes passed 13 checks, and the hosted dispatch and lifecycle suites passed 27. Independent review verified terminal SQL settlement with an inert exit-23 CLI, including capacity release. The collector now restores the v39 predecessor before older source controls. The composed v40 selection, 12 focused collector controls and the installed archive passed in both Python modes. Actual native compilation and hosted verification remain open. [Composed v40 checks](verification/receipts/l05-v40-composed-validation-41a05264.json) [Pre-compiler failure receipt](verification/receipts/l05-starter-preflight-failure-66bba3c2.json)

The standalone and adopted FieldLog variants now have an opt-in local sign-in candidate. Both passed live loopback admission and Swift storage checks for scope, owner isolation, revocation and offline reopening. Both built for arm64 iOS, with no Mixer objects in the baseline. The host tests used an in-memory credential store. Independent review reproduced a matching manual denial that leaves a revoked offline grant open. The correction, installed Keychain and the paired Simulator journey remain pending. [Local sign-in receipt](verification/receipts/fieldlog-d0-d1-local-sign-in-5e584fba.json)

The exact pre-adoption FieldLog revision `9a69cb9` built for arm64 iOS Simulator. Its link map contained zero Mixer products. On macOS, the baseline and adopted repositories read the same workspace and JPEG byte for byte. The adopted app repository committed the offline draft under its original UUID and appended one outbox operation. Local sign-in and the paired Simulator journey remain open. The independent D0 and D1 review verified the public SDK iOS evidence for project creation, entry save, offline draft reopen and photo readback. A local signer stopped during the symbols download before writing an attestation. A fresh signer wrote one envelope that a separate verifier accepted. Both loopback ports and the owned group closed. In-flight worker recovery remains open. [Signer interruption receipt](verification/receipts/l07-signer-interruption-review-5961ea0c.json)

An Android-only host check served all five rebuilt signed releases and resumed the exact interrupted bytes after restart. The native attempt installed the same APKs and Activity Manager returned success. Android kept a System UI unresponsive dialog over FieldLog, ending the run before signed delivery. The rebuilt Android app passed a fresh offline run on API 36. It saved a task, reopened it in a new process, and exported a 168-byte report that a separate app read byte for byte. Two direct VM replays of the rebuilt 240-byte title review exceeded the 50 ms guest deadline. They took 90.5 and 137.9 ms elapsed, used at most 0.274 ms of guest-thread CPU, and made no capability calls. Successful original and rebuilt turns consumed identical fuel. The measurements indicate an off-CPU stall. Scheduler and kernel state remain unobserved. [Host timing receipt](verification/receipts/fieldlog-signed-title-turn-timeout-diagnosis-745406d2.json)

The exact rebuilt FieldLog APK contained all four selected Swift, NDK and ICU notices. The complete archive and both Python test modes passed five checks each. [Runtime notice receipt](verification/receipts/fieldlog-rebuilt-runtime-notices-41a05264.json)

On macOS, one press on the signed Move up button moved File report from third to second. FieldLog advanced revision 8 to 9 and appended one task-order event. A signed FieldLog update on the macOS host changed the Arabic checklist labels. The title and status aligned on the right in RTL and on the left in LTR. The title style change increased its measured height from 16 to 26 points. Changing the macOS accessibility size alone produced no measured growth in either the guest or the native controls. On Android API 36 the signed baseline rendered the complete Arabic title, including 07, at 2× system font scale in RTL. One run displayed a failure while disk already recorded both health milestones for the active launch. A fresh API 36 run verified both healthy releases, fallback after an unsupported asset map, and the updated Arabic title, status and action at 2× RTL. The 1× direction comparison and durable localized tap remain open. The third attempt left its UI dump, private ADB and emulator under a custody hold. The opt-in operation-admission companion migration passed PostgreSQL catalog admission. In the restricted Oban workflow the authenticated POST returned 201. ## Current checkpoint. Public-root SDK support and local end-to-end completion

Main `31f012f13f` defines completion through L01–L08. The public SDK root and nested package now select the unchanged Apple declarations only on macOS hosts. ## Execution priority and blocker escalation

The October 28 user instruction adopts [ADR 0002](docs/adrs/0002-empirical-budgets-and-blocker-experiments.md)
and product-plan sections 20.3/24.4. Advance complete public-SDK FieldLog native
iOS/Android flows alongside VM work; preserve the full hosted and GA scope.
Treat the 64-entry/64-million-cache-credit pair as an experimental baseline.
Prepare the bounded baseline/work-only/capacity comparison and decide from actual
results before extending the existing diagnostic/accounting chain. No experiment
or limit change is claimed by this planning update.

Escalate after two distinct failed approaches, recurrence after a correction, or
two preparation-only cycles; contradicting assumptions and difficult ownership
boundaries escalate immediately. Use fresh Sol 6.2 High subagents for
challenges, experiments, and reviews. Specify bounded ownership, competing
hypotheses, stop conditions, and a named integration decision.

The coordinator selects reversible bounded experiments within existing authority
without another user approval for each variant. Do not turn diagnostic readiness
into full production qualification or recursive review. Preserve failed baselines,
independent promotion review, installed/signed policies and security invariants.
The native/UI/capture pause, disconnected phones, local custody/UnitA holds and
explicit security denials remain active. Each prerequisite names the permitted
command it unlocks or the concrete hold-release condition; historical per-run
freezes do not freeze all later experimental design.

## Product decisions and verification

The iOS minimum is **26.0**, with support intended for iOS 26, 27 and onward.
Record the actual SDK/runtime tested; the private appearance prototype's current
26.5 evidence does not establish broader version qualification. Historical iOS17
compile receipts remain historical. See
[the baseline receipt](verification/ios26-baseline/candidate-1/receipt.json).

Section 8.3 requires the same shared app design on iOS and Android: layout,
hierarchy, colors, typography scale, icons, spacing and component appearance.
Platform-specific app styling requires explicit opt-in. Accessibility,
navigation, text input and system-owned surfaces remain native. Public paths
still need the shared design; existing platform defaults do not satisfy it.
See [the design decision](verification/ui-design-parity-plan-update/receipt.json).

The hosted stack remains Elixir/OTP + Phoenix + Plug/Bandit, Ecto/Postgrex and
Oban on Neon PostgreSQL; Cloudflare Containers for the API, Workers Static Assets
for the SolidJS/TypeScript/Vite/Solid Router console, and Workers/R2/CDN for
suitable edge/delivery workloads. Stripe remains the selected later payment provider. Billing, Checkout and Customer
Portal implementation and activation are deferred; testing and early launch are free. Node is build tooling. Licensed macOS workers and isolated signing retain
necessary hosting exceptions; Android container resources/isolation and Oban
stop/wake/recovery require qualification. Start with direct verified-TLS Neon
connections, bounded pools and production scale-to-zero disabled. Protected Ecto
checkout uses `queue: false` under its selected transaction contract. Existing
Go/local PostgreSQL work is reference/compatibility evidence pending explicit
Phoenix migration. See [the stack decision](verification/hosted-stack-plan-update-2026-10-24/receipt.json).

Implement all product engine code independently. No Patch/Skip implementations,
generated source, tests, fixtures or dependencies. Record general-purpose
libraries in THIRD_PARTY.yml. Existing signed/wire contracts remain strict;
unsupported instructions, capabilities, UI operations or security contracts fail
closed. Static inspection alone never authorizes an OTA release. Keep signing
keys out of source/build workers and use standard cryptography.

Each slice needs executable tests with exact source/tool/command/output scope.
Skipped checks remain pending in their actual scope. FieldLog can complete local
acceptance from pinned public SDK checkout inputs in its separate repository.
Immutable registry packages are later distribution qualification.
Never promote a GA gate without its specified evidence and accountable reviewer.

## Ongoing delivery and resource ownership

**Mixer publishes directly to `origin/main`.** The November 18 user instruction confirms that this repository has no Trunk merge queue. Commit and push each coherent, verified slice promptly, including during long-running plan work. Fetch the current main, incorporate concurrent changes, and use a normal fast-forward push. Check the resulting remote SHA and inspect CI after each push. Report the published SHA, check status, and any work still local.

Root selects the verified paths explicitly and checks the committed file list to preserve other contributors’ staged and unfinished work. Production deployment, store submission, customer messages, destructive migrations and credential changes require separate authorization. [Product plan §34](mixer-product-plan.md#34-how-to-execute-and-maintain-this-plan)

Run independent tasks in parallel with Sol 6.2 High, fresh context, and bounded
file ownership. Coordinate contracts before dependent changes. Keep active lanes
on separate files unless their owners coordinate an edit.

The November 7 direct user instruction resumes native simulator/emulator tests,
necessary owned builds and related UI/capture verification. Use one active owned
instance per lane and shut down disposable instances after collecting evidence,
including failed attempts. Preserve unrelated projects and all specifically held
historical processes, the v5 startup and frozen hosted lanes. Record fresh
attempts separately; earlier pauses and receipts remain historical.

Phones remain disconnected. Do not query/retry phones or request reconnection.
The user accepts simulator/emulator tests for development slices and advancing
dependent work. Run the physical matrix at later hardware/release qualification and correct any
hardware differences then. It does not block local completion. Preserve actual environments and keep final hardware
checks pending. See [the development policy](verification/simulator-development-policy/receipt.json).
